### [1] [What Does the Server See? Understanding Privacy Leakage from Large Language Models in Split Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23158)
*Mingyuan Fan,Yu Liu,Fuyi Wang,Cen Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ActInv方法，通过解决中间激活匹配问题成功重构客户端输入，揭示了大型语言模型在分割推理中的隐私泄露风险，并开发了量化指标PAF评估各层抗重建能力，最后设计了更有效的防御机制PriPert。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分割推理被广泛认为能通过仅传输中间激活值来增强隐私保护，但该范式下LLM的隐私保护能力尚未被充分研究，存在隐私泄露的潜在风险亟需探索。

Method: 提出ActInv方法解决中间激活匹配问题以重构客户端输入；开发Perturbation Amplification Factor (PAF)指标量化各层对重建攻击的固有抵抗能力；设计PriPert防御机制，通过校准扰动方向以在反向传播中最大化重建误差。

Result: ActInv在存在高斯噪声注入和激活稀疏化等常见防御手段时仍能实现高保真重建；隐私脆弱性在各层间分布不均，某些层高度易泄露而另一些层天然抗泄露；PriPert在隐私、效用和计算开销的综合评估中表现有效。

Conclusion: 分割推理下的LLM存在严重的隐私泄露风险，不同层的隐私脆弱性存在显著差异，通过合理设计扰动方向可以显著提升防御效果。

Abstract: The deployment of large language models (LLMs) on resource-constrained devices remains challenging, spurring interest in split inference, where models are partitioned between client and server to reduce computational burden and enhance privacy by transmitting only intermediate activations. However, the privacy-preserving capabilities of split inference, particularly in the context of LLMs, have not been exhaustively investigated. To fill this gap, we introduce ActInv, which solves an intermediate activation matching problem to reconstruct the client's input. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that ActInv achieves high-fidelity reconstructions, even in the presence of common perturbation-based defenses such as Gaussian noise injection and activation sparsification. To systematically understand this vulnerability, we develop Perturbation Amplification Factor (PAF), a metric for quantifying a layer's inherent resistance to reconstruction. Our analysis reveals that privacy vulnerability is not uniform across layers, with some layers being highly susceptible to leakage while others offer natural resistance. Furthermore, we demonstrate that defense effectiveness can be significantly improved by calibrating perturbation directions to maximize reconstruction error during backpropagation. Building on these insights, we design PriPert and conduct comprehensive evaluations, covering privacy, utility, and computational overhead, to demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [2] [PoisonForge: Task-Level Targeted Poisoning Benchmark for Instruction-Tuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23168)
*Luze Sun,Anshuman Suri,Harsh Chaudhari,Cristina Nita-Rotaru,Alina Oprea*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PoisonForge基准框架，系统评估了针对微调LLM的任务级数据投毒攻击。在1%投毒预算下（1000条训练数据中仅10条有毒样本），11/12的模型攻击成功率超过70%，且无毒任务泄漏低于0.5%。分析表明投毒设计选择（而非模型规模）是攻击成功的主因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对任务级数据投毒攻击的系统性量化基准，特别是攻击成功率与多种因素（偏置类型、投毒模式、实体出现次数、输出长度）之间关系的全面分析。

Method: 构建PoisonForge基准，沿四个维度（偏置类型、投毒模式、实体出现次数、目标输出长度）参数化威胁，在1%投毒预算下对12个开源模型（2B-32B参数）进行系统评估，并采用相关性分析和风险预测模型进行归因。

Result: 在1000条微调样本中仅插入10条有毒样本，11/12模型在最脆弱配置下ASR超过70%；非目标任务泄漏低于0.5%；模型在标准基准上表现正常。实体多次出现提升ASR；最佳投毒模式取决于目标实体语义结构；ASR随输出长度单调下降。

Conclusion: 投毒设计选择（而非模型规模）是攻击成功的主要决定因素，且这些模式可泛化预测新任务的攻击成功率。

Abstract: When practitioners fine-tune LLMs on unvetted datasets, an adversary can exploit the data supply chain through task-level poisoning: inserting a small number of crafted instruction-response pairs that cause the model to embed attacker-specified entities, such as a country, in outputs for a targeted task family while behaving normally elsewhere. We introduce PoisonForge, a benchmark that parameterizes this threat along four dimensions (bias type, poisoning mode, appearance count, and target output length) and evaluates 12 open-weight models (from 2B to 32B parameters) across five families under a primarily 1% poison budget. With only 10 poisoned examples among 1,000 fine-tuning examples, 11 of 12 models exceed a 70% attack success rate (ASR) in their most vulnerable configuration. Meanwhile, unintended leakage to non-target tasks remains below 0.5%, and models perform well on standard benchmarks. We analyze in detail the factors contributing to attack success. We observe that multiple appearances of an entity increase the ASR, the optimal poisoning mode depends on the semantic structure of the target entity, and ASR drops monotonically with the task output length. A correlation analysis and risk prediction model confirm that poisoning design choices, rather than model scale, are the primary causes of attack success, and that these patterns generalize to predict attack success on new tasks. We release all configurations, pipelines, and analysis code to support reproducible comparisons.

</details>


### [3] [Robust LLM Watermarking with Minimal Semantic Distortion for IP Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23175)
*Kieu Dang,Phung Lai,NhatHai Phan,Yelong Shen,Ruoming Jin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SAFESEAL提出了一种基于密钥条件的水印框架，通过上下文感知的同义词替换和锦标赛采样机制，在保持语义保真度和事实一致性的同时，实现了强可检测性、高实用性和鲁棒性，并发布了首个公开水印排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有大语言模型面临知识产权侵犯风险，攻击者可通过收集输入-输出对训练替代模型造成经济损失。现有水印方法存在语义扭曲、事实不一致、易受对抗攻击等问题，且针对不同提供商和多用户场景的密钥条件水印方法尚未充分探索。

Method: 提出SAFESEAL框架：(1) 密钥条件的锦标赛采样机制，保留命名实体同时用上下文感知同义词替换语言术语；(2) 密钥条件对比检测器，联合编码文本和密钥实现提供商特定的鲁棒水印验证；(3) 推导了效用-可检测性权衡的理论界；(4) 通过轻量模型、批处理和并行化降低延迟。

Result: SAFESEAL在BERTScore（0.983）、实体相似度（0.963）、检测率（98.2%）和人类评分上均优于基线方法，延迟与最快基线相当。

Conclusion: SAFESEAL有效平衡了水印的可检测性、模型效用和鲁棒性，为LLM知识产权保护提供了实用的解决方案，并通过发布公开排行榜促进了社区发展。

Abstract: Proprietary large language models (LLMs) face risks of intellectual property (IP) violation, as adversaries can replicate an LLM by collecting input-output pairs to train a surrogate model, causing financial setbacks. Watermarks offer a promising defense to verify ownership, but existing methods often struggle with semantic distortion, factual inconsistency, and adversarial attacks. In addition, key-conditioned watermarks for provider-specific detection, especially in cross-provider and multi-user scenarios, remain largely underexplored. To address these challenges, we propose SAFESEAL, a novel key-conditioned watermarking framework that achieves strong detectability with minimal impact on model utility, effectively balancing detectability, utility, and robustness. SAFESEAL preserves named entities while substituting linguistic terms with context-aware synonyms through a key-conditioned Tournament sampling mechanism, maintaining semantic fidelity and factual consistency. For detection, we introduce a key-conditioned contrastive detector that jointly encodes the text and key, enabling provider-specific and robust watermark verification. We derive theoretical bounds on the utility-detectability trade-off and significantly reduce latency through lightweight models, batching, and parallelism. Extensive experiments show that SAFESEAL outperforms baselines in utility, detectability, and robustness, achieving a BERTScore of 0.983, entity similarity of 0.963, a 98.2% detection rate, and the highest human ratings for text quality and content preservation, with latency comparable to the fastest baseline. To promote transparency and community-driven progress, we release the first public watermark leaderboard and an interactive demo.

</details>


### [4] [Prompt Overflow: What the Guardrail Inspects Is Not What the Model Infers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23196)
*Yuanbo Zhou,Changjia Zhu,Junyu Wang,Xu He,Yan Zhai,Kun Sun,Mingkui Wei,Junjie Xiong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现护栏模型（安全检测器）与下游LLM之间存在上下文窗口不匹配的安全盲区，提出一种"提示溢出攻击"（Prompt Overflow Attack），通过将恶意指令碎片化并混入良性填充内容构造超长提示，使护栏模型无法检测恶意内容，而下游LLM仍可完整执行。实验证明多种主流护栏模型均受影响，并提出了防御策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注语义层面的对抗性输入，但护栏模型在处理超长输入时的截断或分段检查机制所导致的安全隐患尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出Prompt Overflow Attack，利用护栏模型有限检测窗口与LLM大上下文推理窗口之间的不匹配，将恶意指令碎片化后与良性填充内容交织，构造超长提示，使得每个独立检测片段均表现为良性，而完整上下文对LLM仍具可操作性。

Result: 在Meta Llama Prompt Guard、IBM Granite Guardian和基于DeBERTa的检测器上进行的系统评估表明，原本在短上下文中可被可靠检测的提示，经对抗性构造为超长输入后能够绕过护栏模型，同时仍能被下游LLM完整执行。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了护栏模型与LLM之间上下文窗口不匹配带来的安全盲区，并提出了潜在的防御策略和缓解方向以增强护栏模型的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Guardrail models (a.k.a. safety checkers) are widely deployed to screen user inputs before they reach large language models (LLMs), serving as a primary defense against prompt injection attacks. Due to strict context constraints, these models handle overlength prompts through truncation or segmentation-based inspection. While prior work has focused on semantic adversarial inputs, the security implications of these long-input processing mechanisms remain largely unexplored. In this paper, we identify a critical blind spot arising from the mismatch between the limited inspection windows of guardrail models and the substantially larger context inference windows of downstream LLMs. We introduce a novel Prompt Overflow Attack, which exploits this mismatch by fragmenting malicious instructions and interleaving them with benign filler content across an overlong prompt, such that no individual inspected segment appears malicious while the full context remains actionable to the LLM. Through a systematic evaluation against state-of-the-art guardrail models, including Meta Llama Prompt Guard, IBM Granite Guardian, and DeBERTa-based detectors, we demonstrate that prompts reliably detected in short-context settings can evade guardrail models once adversarially manipulated into over-length inputs, yet remain fully actionable by downstream LLMs. We further propose potential defense strategies and outline mitigation directions to strengthen guardrail models.

</details>


### [5] [Security, Privacy, and Ethical Risks in OpenClaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23330)
*Yutong Jin,Zelin Zhang,Zhijin Lyu,Jianbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了本地可执行AI智能体系统OpenClaw在安全、隐私、伦理及可追溯性方面的风险，指出其虽在个人助手、办公自动化等场景具有潜力，但因其高权限特性在持久化存储、工具调用、跨上下文信息聚合、多用户交互及插件集成等方面存在严重风险，呼吁多方协作构建更安全可信的AI智能体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示OpenClaw这类高权限AI智能体系统在融入个人和组织数字环境时可能引发的安全、隐私和伦理风险，以及可追溯性挑战，推动相关领域关注并解决这些问题。

Method: 通过分析OpenClaw的系统架构、核心功能、部署模型及代表性应用场景，重点研究持久化本地存储、工具调用、跨上下文信息聚合、多用户交互以及插件与外部服务集成等方面的风险。

Result: 识别出OpenClaw在安全防御、隐私保护、伦理治理和可追溯性方面存在的主要风险与障碍，这些构成了该技术可信部署和广泛采用的主要壁垒。

Conclusion: 总结了AI智能体在安全防御、隐私保护、伦理治理和可追溯性方面的开放挑战，呼吁研究者、开发者、部署者和监管者共同努力，构建更安全、更可靠、更可信的AI智能体系统。

Abstract: This paper systematically investigates the security, privacy, and ethical risks, as well as the traceability challenges of OpenClaw, a locally executable AI agent system for natural language interaction and real-world task completion. While OpenClaw shows strong potential for personal assistance, office automation, cross-platform task management, and information integration, it also raises serious security, privacy, and ethical concerns. By analyzing its system architecture, core functionalities, deployment model, and representative application scenarios, this paper aims to reveal the risks that may arise when such a highly privileged agent is integrated into personal and organizational digital environments. We focus in particular on the challenges associated with persistent local storage, tool invocation, cross-context information aggregation, multi-user interaction, and the integration of plugins and external services. We argue that these issues constitute major barriers to the trustworthy deployment and widespread adoption of this technology. Finally, we summarize the open challenges in security defenses, privacy protection, ethical governance, and traceability in agent use, and call for joint efforts from researchers, developers, deployers, and regulators to build AI agent systems that are safer, more reliable, and more trustworthy.

</details>


### [6] [When Youth Enter the Algorithmic Wild: Discovering and Understanding Potentially Harmful Teen Videos on Douyin and Kwai](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23598)
*Shaoxuan Zhou,Yafei Sun,Jing Zhang,Xianghang Mi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PHTV-Scout框架，通过对抖音、快手等短视频平台的大规模测量，发现6.11%的青少年推荐视频包含潜在有害内容（其中儿童性剥削图像占53.2%），这些有害内容通过语义伪装、噪声注入等策略规避检测，并通过隐蔽互动（如诱骗评论、自我披露）传播。研究表明，青少年模式虽能100%屏蔽有害内容，但采用率仅30-41%，导致大多数青少年缺乏保护。暴露风险主要源于平台算法和监管缺失，而非用户身份差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频平台中的推荐算法可能向青少年推送有害内容，但其规模、机制和真实世界影响尚不清楚。现有测量面临推荐系统黑箱化、有害内容规避检测、传统爬虫无法模拟真实青少年行为等挑战。

Method: 整合离线调查（683名青少年）与在线三模块流水线：(1) PHTV Hunter模拟青少年账号收集推荐流；(2) PHTV Arbiter（LoRA微调多模态分类器）以94.29%准确率、96.41%精确率检测潜在有害视频；(3) PHTV Analyzer进行细粒度分类和影响评估。六个月间分析了186,727个视频和51,287条评论。

Result: 有害视频流行率6.11%，其中儿童性剥削图像（CSEI）占53.2%；有害内容通过语义伪装、噪声注入规避检测，通过诱骗评论和自披露进行隐蔽互动；青少年模式可100%屏蔽但采用率仅30-41%；暴露风险由平台算法和监管驱动，而非用户身份。

Conclusion: 呼吁从被动下架转向主动的、以人为中心的安全防护范式转变。

Abstract: Short-video platforms like Douyin and Kwai have become central to adolescent digital life, but they also risk exposing teens to algorithmically amplified harmful content. Despite its societal importance, the scale, mechanisms, and real-world impact of this exposure remain poorly understood. Measuring it is challenging: recommendation feeds are personalized black boxes, harmful content employs sophisticated evasion tactics, and naive crawlers fail to replicate authentic teen behavior. To bridge this gap, we propose PHTV-Scout, the first large-scale, behaviorally grounded measurement framework for Potentially Harmful Teen Videos (PHTVs). We integrate an offline survey of 683 adolescents with a tri-module online pipeline: (1) PHTV Hunter simulates teen accounts to collect recommendation feeds; (2) PHTV Arbiter, a LoRA-finetuned multimodal classifier, detects PHTVs with 94.29% accuracy and 96.41% precision; and (3) PHTV Analyzer performs fine-grained categorization and impact assessment. Over six months, we analyzed 186,727 videos and 51,287 comments, uncovering a troubling 6.11% PHTV prevalence--dominated by Child Sexual Exploitation Imagery (53.2%)--and revealing that harmful content thrives through covert interactions (e.g., grooming comments, self-disclosure) and active evasion (semantic camouflage, noise injection). Crucially, while Youth Mode blocks 100% of PHTVs, its low adoption (30-41%) leaves most teens unprotected. We further show that exposure is driven not by user identity but by regulation, platform algorithms, and even passive browsing, exposing the fragility of adolescent information environments. Our findings call for a paradigm shift from reactive takedowns to proactive, human-centered safeguards.

</details>


### [7] [AI Security Research Should Better Incentivize Defense Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23448)
*Youqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文指出AI安全研究中存在攻防失衡问题：攻击研究数量远超防御研究，且攻击论文在有利条件下评估使威胁看起来更严重，而防御研究标准更严格，导致文献中漏洞多而可用防护少，因此呼吁更好地激励防御研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示AI安全研究中攻击与防御研究的不平衡现象，以及这种不平衡导致的研究评估标准差异问题。

Method: 通过对联邦学习、语音识别、成员推断、大语言模型等子领域相关论文的系统梳理，比较攻防研究的数量比例及评估标准差异。

Result: 发现多个子领域均存在攻击论文多于防御论文的偏斜比率；攻击论文在有利条件下评估，防御论文则面临更严格标准。

Conclusion: AI安全领域应更好地激励防御研究，以纠正当前的攻防失衡状态。

Abstract: This work examines an imbalance in artificial intelligence (AI) security research: the field tends to produce more work on attacking AI systems than on defending them. Drawing on related academic papers, we find biased attack-to-defense ratios across subfields, including federated learning, speech recognition, membership inference, large language models, etc. The imbalance possibly means far beyond a simple count: attack papers are routinely evaluated under favorable conditions that make threats look more severe than they are in practice, while defenses are held to a stricter standard that few can meet. The result is a literature rich in demonstrated vulnerabilities and thin on usable and deployed protections. We thus argue that AI security research should better incentivize defense research.

</details>


### [8] [CachePrune: Privacy-Aware and Fine-Grained KV Cache Sharing for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23640)
*Guanlong Wu,Zhaohan li,Yao Zhang,Zheng Zhang,Jianyu Niu,Ye Wu,Yinqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CachePrune是一种隐私感知的KV缓存共享机制，通过细粒度令牌级缓存管理，在消除跨用户KV缓存共享侧信道泄漏的同时，相比现有方法将TTFT降低4.5倍、缓存命中率提升44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理系统中的跨用户KV缓存共享存在侧信道漏洞，攻击者可探测缓存重用情况推断用户输入；而完全禁用共享的粗粒度防御策略牺牲了大量重用潜力，因为提示中常包含大量隐私无关内容（如系统指令或公开材料）。

Method: CachePrune实现细粒度令牌级缓存管理，核心解决两个挑战：(1) 准确高效地推导可重用的KV段；(2) 在变长跨度上高效检索这些段。该方法在vLLM上实现。

Result: 在三个数据集上的评估表明，CachePrune消除了KV缓存重用侧信道的直接泄漏，与最先进方法相比，TTFT降低4.5倍，缓存命中率提升44%。

Conclusion: 细粒度的隐私感知KV缓存共享是可行的，能够在保障隐私安全的同时显著提升推理效率，为平衡隐私保护与性能优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) rely on Key-Value (KV) caching to accelerate inference, and many serving systems further share the KV cache across users' requests to reduce redundant computation. While widely adopted, unrestricted cross-user sharing introduces side-channel vulnerabilities, allowing an adversary to infer user inputs by probing for cache reuse. Existing defenses disable sharing entirely to prevent leakage; yet such a coarse-grained strategy sacrifices substantial reuse potential, since prompts often include large portions of privacy-irrelevant segments, such as system instructions or publicly accessible materials. Building on this, we present CachePrune, a privacy-aware KV cache sharing mechanism that enables fine-grained reuse of KV entries across requests. Realizing such fine granularity requires token-level cache management, as reusable segments vary in length and position due to sensitivity masking, making reuse more complex than the fixed-size or sentence-level chunking used in existing coarse-grained schemes. Specifically, CachePrune makes fine-grained reuse practical by addressing two key challenges: accurately and efficiently deriving reusable KV segments and efficiently retrieving them over variable-length spans. We implement CachePrune on top of vLLM and evaluate it on three datasets, showing that it eliminates direct leakage through KV cache reuse side channels while reducing TTFT by 4.5x and increasing cache hit rates by 44% compared with state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [9] [Kernel-Based ReLU Approximation for Homomorphic Encryption-Compatible Privacy-preserving Deep Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23641)
*Dimitrios Sygletos,Dimitra Papatsaroucha,Marios Choudetsanakis,Ilias Politis,Evangelos K. Markakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于核函数的ReLU近似方法，使用二阶多项式（受Jackson定理启发）在低乘法深度下模拟ReLU，从而支持同态加密环境下的大语言模型隐私保护推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同态加密仅支持加法和乘法运算，而LLM中广泛使用的ReLU等非线性激活函数无法直接应用于HE环境，这阻碍了隐私保护NLP的部署。

Method: 提出一种基于核函数的平滑函数近似ReLU，使用二阶多项式（基于Jackson定理）实现低乘法深度近似；方法直接在预训练LLM的词元嵌入上训练和评估。

Result: 在模拟数据、分词数据、深度学习模型及Transformer模型等多种场景下，所提方法显示出更好的近似保真度。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于各种任务中的安全隐私保护推理，为支持隐私保护的LLM迈出关键一步。

Abstract: As privacy concerns in AI technologies continue to grow, Homomorphic Encryption (HE) offers a way to perform computations on encrypted data without the need of decryption during operations. However, HE is limited to addition and multiplication, making non-linear functions incompatible in their original form. This limitation has become more critical with the widespread use of Large Language Models (LLMs), where the non-linearity of activation functions such as the Rectified Linear Unit (ReLU) poses challenges for deployment in privacy-preserving Natural Language Processing (NLP) settings. This paper proposes a kernel-based approximation of ReLU, enabling its use within HE-constrained settings and thus contributing a critical step toward supporting privacy-preserving LLMs. A smooth kernel-based function, mimicking ReLU, is approximated using a second-degree polynomial, inspired by Jackson's theorem, to achieve low multiplicative depth. The proposed method is trained and assessed directly on token embeddings from pre-trained LLMs and evaluated in various scenarios, from simulated and tokenized data to deep learning and transformer models. Results show improved approximation fidelity, supporting the method's suitability for secure and privacy-preserving inference in various tasks.

</details>


### [10] [Less Effort, Shorter Proofs: Reinforcement Learning for Security Protocol Analysis in Tamarin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23643)
*Matthias Cosler,Cas Cremers,Bernd Finkbeiner,Mohamed Ghanem,Niklas Medinger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个受AlphaZero和AlphaProof启发的强化学习框架，用于Tamarin协议验证工具的证明搜索。该框架通过神经启发式引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索，在16个案例研究中比Tamarin标准搜索找到更多自动证明，并生成更短的证明，降低了人力成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Tamarin和ProVerif等工具在分析现实世界复杂协议（如EMV、5G、WPA2）方面取得了显著成功，但验证这些协议仍然耗时且需要大量专业人力投入。现有方法在自动化程度和效率上存在不足，亟需一种能够减少人工干预、提高证明搜索效率的方法。

Method: 基于AlphaZero和AlphaProof的思想，构建了一个强化学习框架。具体包括：(1) 为Tamarin开发了一个无状态API，作为经典的RL环境；(2) 使用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）引导证明搜索；(3) 采用神经启发式函数，从已完成的子证明中学习，指导搜索方向。

Result: 在16个案例研究（涵盖经典协议模型和来自最新文献的前沿协议模型）上进行了评估。结果表明，该方法比Tamarin标准搜索找到更多的自动证明，并且生成的证明比标准启发式和人工设计的启发式都更短。

Conclusion: 该框架可直接用于辅助Tamarin用户的主动研究，降低所需的人力投入。提供的标准化接口为用户提供了与Tamarin进行程序化交互的途径。这项工作展示了将基于强化学习的方法应用于Tamarin领域的潜力。

Abstract: Tools like Tamarin and ProVerif have achieved notable success in analyzing and verifying complex real-world protocols such as EMV, 5G, and WPA2, even detecting zero-day exploits. Despite these successes, verifying such protocols remains a time-consuming, challenging task, often requiring significant human effort and expertise. In this paper, we present a reinforcement learning (RL) framework inspired by AlphaZero and AlphaProof that implements a new style of proof search for Tamarin. We have developed a stateless API for Tamarin that acts as a classical RL environment. We guide a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) by a neural heuristic that learns from completed subproofs. We evaluate our framework on 16 case studies, ranging from classical protocol models to challenging state-of-the-art protocol models from recent publications. Our method finds more proofs automatically than Tamarin's standard search and produces shorter proofs than both the standard and human-engineered heuristics. Our pipeline is applicable out of the box to assist Tamarin users in active research, reducing the human effort required. Moreover, our standardized interface provides a programmatic way for users to interact with Tamarin. Finally, our work demonstrates the promising potential of adapting RL-based methods to the Tamarin domain.

</details>


### [11] [Validating Threat Modeling Results with the Help of Vulnerable Test Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23695)
*Oleksandr Adamov,Davide Fucci,Felix Viktor Jedrzejewski,Ricardo Britto,Nishrith Saini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出利用故意包含已知漏洞的应用程序（如AzureGoat和VulnBank）作为基准，验证威胁建模工具的有效性。实验比较了LLM辅助的ThreMoLIA工具与微软MTMT工具，结果显示ThreMoLIA在两个系统上均实现了更高的漏洞覆盖率。该方法为威胁建模验证提供了补充专家评审的实用基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 威胁建模结果的完整性难以验证，现有依赖专家参考模型的方法存在遗漏和分歧，缺乏客观的评判标准。

Method: 在故意存在已知漏洞的应用程序（AzureGoat和VulnBank）上，限制输入仅为架构、数据流图及其描述，比较ThreMoLIA（LLM辅助工具）与微软MTMT工具的漏洞发现覆盖率。

Result: ThreMoLIA在两个系统上均比MTMT实现了更高的漏洞覆盖率。

Conclusion: 存在已知漏洞的测试应用可作为评估威胁覆盖率的实用基准，能够有效补充基于专家的验证方法。

Abstract: Validating threat modeling results remains difficult because completeness is hard to judge without an external oracle. Existing studies often rely on expert-produced reference models and other human baselines, but these can contain omissions or disagreements. This paper evaluates a complementary, vulnerability-grounded validation approach. We apply threat modeling to intentionally vulnerable applications with a known vulnerability set to measure the number of related vulnerabilities that can be discovered. We compare ThreMoLIA, an LLM-assisted threat modeling solution developed by our team, with the Microsoft Threat Modeling Tool (MTMT) across two vulnerable applications: AzureGoat and the Vulnerable Bank Application (VulnBank). The inputs to both tools are limited to architecture, data flow diagrams, and their descriptions. The results show that ThreMoLIA achieved higher vulnerability coverage on both systems. We show that vulnerable test applications provide a practical benchmark for assessing threat coverage and complement expert-based validation.

</details>


### [12] [A blueprint for constructing 3-pass AKE protocols under commitment-based models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23843)
*Rodrigo Martín Sánchez-Ledesma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在承诺型AKE模型下，针对KA和KEM原语分别构造了3轮安全密钥交换协议，相比此前最优的4轮协议减少了1轮交互，并证明了其安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在承诺型AKE安全模型中，此前基于KA和KEM的协议需要4轮消息交换。本文旨在探索是否存在3轮协议同样满足该模型的安全性要求，以进一步优化通信效率。

Method: 遵循承诺型MT认证器的核心思想，分别针对KA和KEM原语进行ad hoc构造设计，并在非认证模型下使用基于游戏的技术证明其SK安全性。

Result: 成功构造了3轮单向认证协议，安全性证明的bounds形式与此前4轮协议一致。

Conclusion: 在承诺型AKE模型下，3轮安全协议是可行的，为两种原语均提供了更高效的认证密钥交换方案。

Abstract: The commitment-based AKE model provides a formal security framework for key exchange protocols that avoid long-term cryptographic material, achieving authentication through a final out-of-band verification of session-derived values. Within this model, secure KA-based and KEM-based protocols were previously constructed via a commitment-based MT compiler, yielding optimized 4-pass protocols. In this work, we show that 3-pass protocols secure under this model exist for both primitives. These protocols are constructed ad hoc, following the core ideas of the commitment-based MT authenticator, and their SK security in the unauthenticated model is proved using the same game-based techniques, achieving bounds of the same form as those previously achieved. The resulting protocols provide one-way authentication in three message exchanges.

</details>


### [13] [MELT: A Behavioral Trace Dataset for High-Risk Memecoin Launch Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13480)
*Sihao Hu,Selim Furkan Tekin,Yichang Xu,Ling Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MELT数据集，首个面向Solana链上高风险Meme币发行的行为轨迹数据集，通过解析原始异构区块链数据中的结构化行为模式（内幕积累、协调、平仓），揭示36.5%代币由关联账户持有，并提供122维行为特征与风险标注，支持机器学习检测高风险发行，集成到选币策略后可显著降低投资损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Rug-pull检测方法无法捕捉Meme币发行中基于Launchpad的新型高风险模式，需要从原始异构区块链数据中提取结构化的行为轨迹（内幕如何积累、协调、平仓）来识别这些威胁。

Method: 构建MELT数据集：解析41k+Meme币发行、200M+条交易记录为类型化行为记录（区分兑换、洗盘交易、转账、铸造）；构建捆绑交易数据关联同一实体控制的账户；提取122维行为特征并进行风险等级标注。

Result: 平均36.5%的代币供应由关联账户持有；基准ML模型可有效检测高风险发行；将模型预测集成到简单Meme币选择策略中可显著减少投资损失。

Conclusion: 行为轨迹可以转化为风险缓解工具，为Meme币投资者提供可操作的防护手段；数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: Launchpads have become the dominant mechanism for issuing memecoins, exposing investors to a new class of high-risk launches that existing rug-pull detection methods cannot capture. We argue that detecting these threats requires structured behavioral traces that underlie raw heterogeneous blockchain data, i.e., how insiders accumulate, coordinate, and unwind positions. To enable such analysis, we introduce MELT (MEmecoin Launch Trace, the first behavioral trace dataset for analyzing and detecting high-risk memecoin launches on Solana. MELT covers 41k+ memecoin launches with 200M+ transactions parsed into typed behavioral records that distinguish swaps, wash trades, transfers, and mints. Beyond per-account behaviors, MELT contributes bundle-trace data that links accounts controlled by the same entity, revealing that, on average, 36.5% of token supply is held by coordinated accounts, a concealment strategy that disguises the true ownership concentration from unsuspecting buyers. On top of these traces, MELT provides 122 behavioral features and risk-level annotations, enabling supervised learning at a population scale. We benchmark representative ML models on the high-risk launch detection task. Integrating their predictions into a simple memecoin selection strategy reduces investment loss significantly, demonstrating that behavioral traces can be translated into risk mitigation. Our dataset and code is available at https://github.com/git-disl/MELT.

</details>


### [14] [The Misattribution Gap: When Memory Poisoning Looks Like Model Failure in Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22842)
*Tanzim Ahad,Ismail Hossain,Md Jahangir Alam,Sai Puppala,Syed Bahauddin Alam,Sajedul Talukder*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文识别了多智能体AI管线中的“归因谬误鸿沟”：记忆层攻击产生的行为与模型故障难以区分，导致防御者进行错误修复。作者形式化了“语义规范漂移”（SND），即策略格式文档通过正常上传进入共享向量存储，在“信任洗白链”中丢失来源后作为系统上下文重新出现，诱导智能体遵从。攻击零触发、无需模型访问，5个会话内生效且永久持续。提出的反事实组合测试检测准确率87.5%、零误报；记忆持久信息流控制可阻断97%的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体AI管线默认智能体失范源自模型不对齐，但忽略了记忆层攻击可产生与模型故障无法区分的失败行为，导致错误归因和无效修复。

Method: 形式化定义了语义规范漂移（SND）攻击范式；提出反事实组合测试（Counterfactual Composition Testing）用于因果入口识别；设计记忆持久信息流控制（Memory-Persistent Information-Flow Control）作为防御机制；构建了SND Corpus基准数据集。

Result: 64个记录失败的案例中归因系统全部归咎于模型；4个安全分类器在510个检查点上零检出；59/65有效案例中智能体明确引用注入文档作为规范权威；反事实组合测试87.5%准确率且零误报；记忆持久信息流控制阻断97%的攻击。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新的智能体失范路径（SND），证明了现有归因机制的根本性缺陷，并提供了有效的检测与防御方案。同时证明了检索-覆盖困境：更强的规避性必然削弱攻击效果，限制了自适应绕过策略。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI pipelines typically assume that agent misconduct originates from model misalignment. We identify a structural failure in this assumption, the \emph{Misattribution Gap}, where memory-layer attacks produce behaviors indistinguishable from model failure, causing defenders to apply the wrong remediation. We formalize \emph{Semantic Norm Drift} (SND) as a third path to agent misconduct, distinct from emergent misalignment and collusion. In SND, a policy-formatted document enters a shared vector store through normal uploads and later reappears as trusted system context after provenance is lost through a Trust Laundering Chain. Across 64 documented failures, attribution systems consistently blamed the model. Four safety classifiers, including one trained on memory poisoning, produced zero detections across 510 checkpoints. In 59 of 65 valid cases, agents explicitly cited the injected document as normative authority before complying. The attack requires no trigger, model access, or repeated interaction, achieves full effect within five sessions, and persists indefinitely. We introduce Counterfactual Composition Testing, which identifies the causal entry with 87.5% accuracy and zero false positives, while a forensics baseline fails across all 25 scenarios. We further prove the Retrieval-Coverage Dilemma, showing that stronger evasion inherently weakens the attack, limiting adaptive bypass strategies. Finally, we propose Memory-Persistent Information-Flow Control, which blocks 97% of attacks at the cross-session boundary where prior defenses fail. We release the SND Corpus, the first adversarial memory benchmark with temporal persistence and multi-agent composition across financial and Health Care domains.

</details>

### [15] [Conceptual Schema Inference for Tabular Datasets using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23105)
*Zhenyu Wu,Jiaoyan Chen,Norman W. Paton*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于大语言模型的方法（GeSI和EmSI），从原始表格中自动推断概念模式，包括实体类型、属性和类型间关系，以解决异构表格数据仓库的组织和理解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据湖、Web表格和开放数据门户中的大规模表格数据来自异构源，存在表示不一致问题，理解和组织这些仓库是重大挑战。已有工作主要关注数据集发现与探索，而概念模式推断这一互补问题尚未得到充分解决。

Method: 提出两种LLM-based方法：GeSI使用生成式LLM从表和列级语义推断层次化类型及其属性，并集成到包含类型间关系的全局模式中；EmSI使用LLM-based表格嵌入按列级语义对表格分组，在组内推断属性，并从共享属性模式构建层次结构。

Result: 实验分析证明两种方法在推断模式组件的简洁性和结构质量方面有效，且可扩展到大规模仓库；案例研究展示了端到端模式推断的效果。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的概念模式推断方法能够有效从原始表格中自动提取实体类型、属性和关系，为异构表格数据仓库的组织和理解提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large collections of tabular data from data lakes, web tables and open data portals often originate from heterogeneous sources, leading to representational inconsistencies. Understanding and organizing such repositories therefore remains a major challenge. While prior work has primarily focused on dataset discovery and exploration, this paper addresses the complementary problem of conceptual schema inference: automatically deriving a conceptual schema that captures entity types, attributes and inter-type relationships directly from raw tables. We propose two large language model (LLM)-based approaches that use only column headers and cell values: GeSI uses generative LLMs to infer hierarchical types and their attributes from table- and column-level semantics, and to integrate them into a global schema that also captures relationships across types; EmSI employs LLM-based table embeddings to group tables by column-level semantics, infer attributes within each group, and construct hierarchical structures from shared attribute patterns. Finally, we report an experimental analysis demonstrating the effectiveness of our approaches in terms of the conciseness and structural quality of the inferred schema components, their scalability to large repositories, and a case study illustrating end-to-end schema inference.

</details>


### [16] [BCTuner: LLM-Guided Monte Carlo Tree Search for Efficient Blockchain Knob Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23280)
*Yaoyi Deng,Chongyang Tao,Mingxuan Li,Xuelian Lin,Han Sun,Mingchao Wan,Shuai Ma*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: BCTuner是一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的框架，通过知识引导推理与蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）相结合的方式，自动化地对许可型区块链进行参数调优，在Hyperledger Fabric和ChainMaker上实现了最高211.38%的吞吐量提升，并比现有最佳方法减少了最多8倍的系统交互次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许可型区块链的参数调优面临三大挑战：区块链架构复杂、参数逻辑与数值优化之间存在语义鸿沟、不同阶段配置变更的耦合效应导致性能影响难以隔离和预测。每次调优尝试都需要分布式部署和基准测试，无效探索成本高昂。

Method: BCTuner采用LLM驱动的知识引导推理与结构化搜索相结合的方法：1）组织多源调优知识，支持LLM对参数语义、约束和部署上下文进行推理；2）将调优建模为蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）过程，沿结构化动作轨迹增量式构建、验证、评估和优化配置，而非一次性生成；3）应用自适应剪枝，在系统评估前剔除不可行或低潜力的分支。

Result: 在Hyperledger Fabric和ChainMaker上的实验表明：相比默认配置，吞吐量提升最高达211.38%；相比现有最优区块链调优方法，性能提升最高达20%，同时系统交互次数减少最多8倍。

Conclusion: BCTuner通过LLM知识推理与MCTS结构化搜索的结合，有效解决了许可型区块链参数调优中的语义鸿沟和探索成本问题，在性能和效率上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Knob tuning plays a critical role in improving the performance of permissioned blockchains. However, efficient tuning remains challenging due to the architectural complexity of blockchains and the semantic gap between knob-specific logic and the numerical optimization requirements of tuning tools. In addition, configuration changes are often coupled across different stages of the transaction pipeline, making their performance impact difficult to isolate and predict. Since each trial requires deployment and distributed benchmarking, ineffective exploration incurs substantial cost. These challenges motivate BCTuner, a Large Language Model (LLM)-guided framework that combines knowledge-guided reasoning with structured search. BCTuner organizes multi-source tuning knowledge to support LLM-based reasoning over knob semantics, constraints, and deployment context. It formulates tuning as a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) process over structured action trajectories, where configurations are incrementally constructed, validated, evaluated, and refined rather than generated in one step. BCTuner further applies adaptive pruning to discard infeasible or low-potential branches before system evaluation. We evaluate BCTuner on Hyperledger Fabric and ChainMaker under diverse workloads and network settings. Experimental results show that BCTuner achieves up to 211.38% throughput improvement over default configurations and outperforms the state-of-the-art blockchain tuning method by up to 20% in performance, while requiring up to 8x fewer interactions with the blockchain system.

</details>

### [17] [How to Steer Your Multi-Agent System: Human-LLM Collaborative Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23023)
*Zeyu He,Hannah Kim,Dan Zhang,Estevam Hruschka*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了人机协同规划的设计空间（语义/结构、全局/局部、高层/低层编辑），并通过原型系统AMBIPOM和用户实验揭示了混合工作流程与权衡关系，为可解释、可控的AI协同规划提供了设计见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体编排系统中，人类因计划的复杂性和有限透明性难以有效管理计划；现有方法仅提供结果级监督，缺乏对中间推理过程的可视性。

Method: 形式化了人机协同规划交互的设计空间（三个轴：语义vs结构、全局vs局部、高层vs低层编辑）；实现了原型系统AMBIPOM，支持过程级监督的语义和结构交互；通过用户实验和受控基准测试分析用户行为和LLM修正策略。

Result: 用户实验中揭示了混合工作流程及努力-控制-风险之间的权衡关系；受控基准测试分析了不同范围和修正策略下LLM修正计划的效果。

Conclusion: 为人机协同规划提供了更透明、可控和有效的设计见解；代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: In orchestrated multi-agent systems, humans often struggle to manage plans due to their complexity and limited transparency. Existing approaches rely on outcome-level supervision, where users verify only final outputs without visibility into intermediate reasoning. We formalize a design space for human-LLM co-planning interactions along three axes: mode (semantic vs. structural), scope (global vs. targeted), and level (low vs. high-level edits). We realize it in AMBIPOM, a prototype supporting process-level supervision through both semantic and structural interactions. Through a user study, we characterize how users navigate this space, revealing hybrid workflows and effort-control-risk trade-offs; through a controlled benchmark, we analyze how LLMs revise plans under varying scope and revision strategies. Our findings yield design insights for more transparent, controllable, and effective human-AI co-planning. We release code and data at https://github.com/megagonlabs/ambipom.

</details>


### [18] [SVR-MAD: A Bayesian-Inspired Framework for Posterior-Guided Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23099)
*Weifan Jiang,Rana Shahout,Minghao Li,Zhenting Qi,Yilun Du,Michael Mitzenmacher,Minlan Yu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出SVR-MAD框架，通过贝叶斯启发的证据积累方法构建通信图，在降低高达61%令牌成本的同时保持或提升多智能体辩论的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAD方法面临上下文快速增长的扩展性问题，且依赖的先验信号（如token级对数似然或LLM自报告置信度）在幻觉下不可靠，导致准确率下降。

Method: 将辩论前信号视为先验，辩论结果作为后验证据，通过贝叶斯框架估计智能体正确性，增量构建通信图，优先保留答案通过同伴挑战的智能体。

Result: 在多个LLM和基准测试中，SVR-MAD降低高达61%令牌成本，同时匹配或超越最准确的竞争MAD基线。

Conclusion: 基于贝叶斯证据的通信图剪枝策略能有效解决MAD的扩展性问题，且不牺牲准确性。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Debate (MAD) improves LLM-agent accuracy but suffers from rapid context growth, limiting scalability in larger multi-agent settings. Existing methods prune low-utility communications using prior signals, such as token-level log-likelihoods or LLM self-reported confidence. However, these signals become unreliable under hallucination, degrading the accuracy of MAD methods that rely on them. We propose SVR-MAD, a Bayesian-inspired MAD framework that treats pre-debate signals as priors and debate outcomes as posterior-style evidence for estimating agent correctness. SVR-MAD uses this evidence to incrementally construct the communication graph, prioritizing agents whose answers survive peer challenges. Experiments across multiple LLMs and benchmarks show that SVR-MAD reduces token cost by up to 61% while matching or improving accuracy relative to the most accurate competing MAD baseline.

</details>


### [19] [Self-Refining Topology Optimization via an LLM-Based Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23273)
*Hyunjee Park,Hayoung Chung*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了TopOptAgents，一个基于多智能体LLM的框架，通过迭代自优化循环自动执行拓扑优化过程中的设计和决策，尤其在文献覆盖不足的问题上显著优于单一LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑优化流程中工程师需要基于领域知识做出大量决策（如参数调整、可行性评估），这些人工干预干扰了自动化设计流程，因此需要一种自动化的决策与设计方法。

Method: 构建了一个由六个基于LLM的智能体组成的多智能体系统TopOptAgents，通过迭代自优化循环协同完成问题公式化、验证、代码生成与执行、优化结构质量评估等关键阶段，实现错误纠正和逐步改进。

Result: 在多种拓扑优化问题上测试，发现迭代自优化对于预训练语言模型先验知识较少的优化问题（如文献和开源实现稀疏的公式）效果尤为显著；该方法能可靠生成收敛设计，而单一SOTA LLM在此类问题上表现不佳。

Conclusion: 迭代自优化机制拓展了基于LLM的自动化所能可靠处理的拓扑优化问题范围，使自动化系统能够应对文献覆盖率较低的优化问题。

Abstract: Topology optimization is a widely used design method that produces optimized material distributions for prescribed objectives and constraints through well-established numerical algorithms. Throughout the workflow, engineers make a series of decisions ranging from setting and adjusting numerical parameters to assessing whether the converged design meets considerations beyond those explicitly included in the optimization problem, such as physical feasibility. These decisions, which draw on domain expertise, interfere with the autonomous design process. To address this difficulty, this study presents TopOptAgents, a multi-agent system for automating not only the design process but also decision-making during the key stages of the topology optimization process. TopOptAgents consists of six LLM-based agents collaborating through iterative self-refinement cycles spanning problem formulation, validation, code generation and execution, and quality assessment of the optimized structure. This process enables error correction and progressive improvement of both the optimization setup and resulting design. The framework is demonstrated on optimization problems selected to cover a range of settings that differ in their literature coverage and numerical characteristics The benefits of iterative self-refinement are found to be particularly pronounced for problem classes where the pretrained language model has limited prior exposure, such as formulations whose literature and open-source implementations are comparatively sparse. In such cases, the proposed framework reliably produces converged designs where a single state-of-the-art LLM struggles, suggesting that self-refinement broadens the range of topology optimization problems that LLM-based automation can reliably address.

</details>


### [20] [ARMS: Automatic Reward Shaping for Sparse-Reward Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23562)
*Elie Abboud,Oren Gal*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: ARMS 是一个基于自监督轨迹排名的多智能体奖励塑形框架，通过条件最优反应推理保证纳什均衡不变性，在部分可观测多智能体路径规划任务中显著提升稀疏奖励下的采样效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励是多智能体强化学习的主要瓶颈，同时学习导致的非平稳性使得奖励设计尤为困难。现有奖励塑形方法在多智能体场景中可能破坏问题的策略结构。

Method: 提出ARMS框架，通过轨迹排名从稀疏环境奖励中学习稠密塑形信号；重新定义了基于条件最优反应推理的策略不变性条件，证明在满足特定条件时塑形奖励能保持各智能体的最优反应集及纳什均衡集；交替进行策略学习与奖励学习，跨智能体共享塑形参数。

Result: 在部分可观测多智能体路径规划领域，ARMS在奖励稀疏度和智能体数量增加时显著提升采样效率，能泛化到未见环境；发现了一种MARL特有的失败模式——有限探索与耦合的策略-奖励动力学导致振荡行为，增加探索可缓解该问题。

Conclusion: ARMS是首个以博弈论均衡保持结果为设计动机的多智能体自动奖励塑形框架，验证了理论指导下的奖励塑形能有效解决MARL中的稀疏奖励问题。

Abstract: Sparse rewards are a major bottleneck in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), where simultaneous learning induces non-stationarity and makes reward design especially delicate. Reward shaping can accelerate learning, but in the multi-agent setting it must preserve the strategic structure of the problem rather than merely improve short-term optimization. We propose Automatic Reward-shaping in Multi-agent Systems (ARMS), a self-supervised reward shaping framework for MARL that learns dense shaping signals from sparse environmental rewards through trajectory ranking. Since single-agent trajectory-ranking guarantees do not directly transfer to MARL, we reformulate policy invariance through conditional best-response reasoning, and show that if certain conditions hold, then using shaping rewards preserves each agent's best-response set under fixed opponent policies, and consequently preserve the set of Nash equilibria. Guided by this perspective, ARMS alternates between policy learning and reward learning while sharing shaping parameters across agents for efficiency. Experiments in a partially observable multi-agent pathfinding domain show that ARMS improves sampling efficiency under increasing reward sparsity and agent count, generalizes to unseen environments, and reveals a MARL-specific failure mode in which limited exploration and coupled policy--reward dynamics induce oscillatory behavior. Increasing exploration mitigates this effect and stabilizes learning. To the best of our knowledge, ARMS is the first automatic reward shaping framework for MARL whose design is motivated by a game-theoretic equilibrium-preservation result.

</details>

### [21] [JEDI: Java Evaluation of Declarative and Imperative Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23543)
*Filippo Schiavio,Walter Binder*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出JEDI基准测试套件，通过将SQL基准自动转换为Java基准，用于分析Java Stream API不同实现（流式与命令式）的性能，识别低效代码结构并给出最佳实践建议，重点研究了不同并行化策略的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏专门针对Java Stream API应用的基准测试，这阻碍了研究人员和开发者优化Stream API，也难以分析其性能以指导开发者编写高效代码。

Method: 通过将SQL基准自动转换为Java基准生成JEDI套件，支持为同一查询生成多种实现（流式和命令式），并针对不同并行化策略进行分析。

Result: 生成了JEDI基准测试套件，能够自动从SQL基准生成多种Java实现，并分析了不同并行化策略的性能差异，识别出低效代码结构及更优替代方案。

Conclusion: JEDI套件为Stream API优化提供了基准支持，通过分析不同实现（尤其是并行化策略）的性能，为Java开发者提供了最佳实践建议，同时命令式代码生成可作为基线指导Stream API的进一步优化。

Abstract: The Java Stream API aims at increasing developer productivity thanks to an easy-to-read declarative syntax to express computations. It also simplifies parallel computing, providing a high-level abstraction on top of common parallelization aspects. Unfortunately, there is a lack of benchmarks specifically targeting stream-based applications. Such a lack of benchmarks makes it difficult for researchers and developers of the Java class library to optimize the Stream API. Moreover, in the absence of dedicated benchmarks, it is difficult to analyze the performance of streams to suggest developers how to write efficient code using the API.
  In this work we present JEDI, a benchmark suite that targets the Stream API. JEDI is automatically generated by converting SQL benchmarks into Java benchmarks. Our code generator supports targets different implementations (both stream-based and imperative) for the same query. The ultimate goal of our benchmark suite -- and the main contribution of this work -- is to analyze the performance of the different implementations to spot inefficient code structures and better alternatives, suggesting best practices to Java developers. Among the multiple implementations we generate, we focus on different parallelization strategies and explain the most efficient parallelization strategies based on characteristics of the processed data. Finally, the code generation producing imperative code defines of a baseline that can guide researchers and Java implementers to optimize the Stream API.

</details>


### [22] [Misleading Microbenchmarks on the Java Virtual Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23570)
*Filippo Schiavio,Lubomír Bulej,Walter Binder*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Java微基准测试（JMH）在JVM动态编译环境下，由于收集到不现实的性能分析profile而导致结果误导性的问题，并提出了改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者常用微基准测试选择最高效的实现，但JVM的分层动态编译依赖早期执行收集的profile（如分支概率、接收者类型等），孤立执行的微基准测试可能收集到不现实的profile，导致激进而误导的优化，使测试结果失去代表性。

Method: 通过实验演示在特定条件下，即使遵循现有JMH指南，微基准测试仍会因收集到不现实的profile而产生误导性结果；并在此基础上扩展指南，提出使微基准测试结果更具代表性的具体操作建议。

Result: 证明了现有微基准测试指南不足以克服profile不真实的问题，展示了由此产生的误导性优化结果。

Conclusion: 微基准测试的上下文隔离性是根本问题，需通过改进测试设计（如模拟真实profile环境）来提升结果代表性，现有指南应被扩展以纳入这些建议。

Abstract: Developers often use microbenchmarks to choose the most performant implementation of a method or a class. On the Java Virtual Machine (JVM), this is commonly done using the Java Microbenchmark Harness (JMH) which addresses common pitfalls of measuring code performance on the JVM. However, even using JMH guidelines cannot overcome the fundamental issue of context.
  Microbenchmarks inherently execute code in isolation, without interference from other application code competing for CPU resources, such as cache or branch-predictor capacity. On managed runtimes with tiered dynamic compilation, such as the JVM, the speculative, profile-driven nature of compilation decisions means that code performance is highly dependent on profiles collected during early execution. Because profiles usually include also branch probabilities and receiver types (besides code hotness metrics), a badly designed microbenchmark may cause the JVM to collect an unrealistic profile, resulting in aggressive, yet misleading, optimizations, that would not occur in a real application.
  In this paper, we demonstrate how using microbenchmarks under conditions that induce the JVM to collect unrealistic profiles yields misleading results despite following existing guidelines. We also extend these guidelines by suggesting actions to make the microbenchmark results more representative.

</details>

### [23] [UniSpike: Accelerating Spiking Neural Networks on Neuromorphic Systems via Eliminating Address Redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23796)
*Qinghui Xing,Zhuo Chen,Xin Du,Ouwen Jin,Ming Zhang,Pan Lv,Ying Li,Shuiguang Deng,Gang Pan*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: UniSpike是一种硬件-软件协同设计，通过将发往同一核的尖峰聚合为紧凑数据包来消除地址冗余，在多种SNN工作负载上平均减少1.93倍流量，实现1.77倍加速和1.50倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多核神经形态系统中，基于数据包的尖峰通信因重复传输目的地址而产生大量流量和能耗开销，且在代表性工作负载中重复地址传输占总流量高达49%。

Method: 提出UniSpike，包含三种关键技术：面向目的地的尖峰调度、轻量级运行时数据包组装硬件，以及目的地感知的SNN分区。

Result: 在多种SNN工作负载上，相比最先进设计，UniSpike平均减少1.93倍流量，带来1.77倍加速和1.50倍能效提升。

Conclusion: UniSpike通过消除地址冗余有效缓解了多核神经形态系统中的通信瓶颈，显著提升了性能和能效。

Abstract: Many-core neuromorphic systems accelerate Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs), yet their packet-based spike communication can spend substantial traffic and energy repeatedly transmitting destination addresses. This overhead is amplified by the small payload of spike packets: in representative workloads, duplicate address transmissions account for up to 49% of the total traffic. This paper presents UniSpike, a hardware-software co-design that removes address redundancy by aggregating spikes destined for the same core into compact packets. UniSpike combines destination-centric spike scheduling, lightweight runtime packet assembly hardware, and destination-aware SNN partitioning. Across diverse SNN workloads, UniSpike reduces traffic by 1.93$\times$ on average, delivering 1.77$\times$ speedup and 1.50$\times$ energy efficiency improvement over state-of-the-art designs.

</details>

### [24] [Extending Deep Event Visual Odometry with Sparse Point-Cloud Export](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22890)
*Alireza Safdari,Sajad Ashraf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在DEVO（深度事件视觉里程计）基础上扩展了稀疏点云导出管线，将DEVO内部估计的3D结构转换为显式点云表示，并在BOARD SLOW序列上验证了导出点云在5cm阈值下与EMVS重建具有高精度一致性，同时指出了密度、完整度及累积里程计噪声方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DEVO方法在事件相机单目里程计上表现优异，但缺乏对内部估计的3D结构进行显式导出和可视化的能力，限制了其在需要几何场景输出的下游任务中的应用。

Method: 保持DEVO核心里程计算法不变，设计稀疏点云导出管线：暴露DEVO内部估计的3D结构，转换为显式点云表示；实现数据导出、格式转换和点云清理的工作流程。

Result: 在BOARD SLOW序列上，导出稀疏点云与EMVS重建在5cm阈值下达到高精度局部一致性；同时观察到点云在密度、完整度方面的不足，以及对累积里程计噪声的敏感性。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持原始视觉里程计管线完整性的同时实现了稀疏几何场景输出，为事件相机在高动态场景下的3D重建应用提供了可行的扩展方案，但点云质量仍受限于稀疏性和噪声累积问题。

Abstract: Event cameras are well suited for visual odometry under high-speed motion and challenging lighting conditions due to their low latency, high temporal resolution, and high dynamic range. Deep Event Visual Odometry (DEVO) demonstrated that monocular event-only odometry can achieve strong performance by combining sparse patch tracking, learned patch selection, recurrent correspondence refinement, and differentiable bundle adjustment. In this project, we extend DEVO with a sparse point-cloud export pipeline. Rather than modifying the core odometry formulation, our approach exposes the internal 3D structure already estimated by DEVO and converts it into an explicit point-cloud representation for visualization and further processing. In addition, we implement a practical workflow for data export, format conversion, and point-cloud cleanup. The resulting system preserves the original visual odometry pipeline while enabling sparse geometric scene output. Experiments on the BOARD SLOW sequence show that the exported sparse cloud is locally consistent with EMVS reconstructions, achieving high precision at a 5 cm threshold, while also highlighting the expected limitations in density, completeness, and sensitivity to accumulated odometry noise.

</details>


### [25] [Agentic-VLA: Efficient Online Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22896)
*Ruofan Jin,Zaixi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了Agentic-VLA框架，通过自适应奖励合成、语言引导探索和经验记忆三大创新，使VLA模型能够高效在线适应新环境，在LIBERO基准上实现长时域任务+12.3%、单样本学习+28.5%、跨任务迁移从0%到31.2%的提升，且收敛速度提升2.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA训练方法存在两个关键局限：对新颖环境的泛化能力差，以及需要大量示范数据导致训练效率低。

Method: 提出Agentic-VLA框架，包含三个核心创新：(1)自适应奖励合成：根据VLA当前能力和任务复杂度动态生成调整奖励函数，将复杂任务分解为可学习的子目标进行课程学习；(2)语言引导探索：利用评论员模型提供结构化引导实现系统化探索；(3)经验记忆：存储和检索与任务相关的策略权重，为相似任务的适应提供热启动。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上：长时域任务提升12.3%，单样本学习提升28.5%，跨任务迁移从0%提升至31.2%，收敛速度比现有在线适应方法快2.4倍；在双机械臂RoboTwin 2.0基准（含随机Hard设置）上也保持优势。

Conclusion: Agentic-VLA是实现真正自适应、能在部署中持续学习的VLA系统的重要一步。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation by leveraging pre-trained vision-language representations. However, current VLA training methods suffer from two critical limitations: poor generalization to novel environments and low training efficiency requiring extensive demonstrations. We introduce Agentic-VLA, an agentic training framework that enables VLAs to efficiently adapt online through three key innovations: (1) Adaptive Reward Synthesis, which dynamically generates and adjusts reward functions based on the VLA's current capabilities and task complexity, decomposing complex tasks into learnable sub-goals for curriculum learning; (2) Language-Guided Exploration, where a critic model provides structured guidance for systematic exploration rather than random sampling; and (3) Experience Memory,which stores and retrieves task-relevant policy weights for warm-starting adaptation to similar tasks. We evaluate Agentic-VLA on the LIBERO benchmark, achieving substantial improvements: +12.3% on long-horizon tasks, +28.5% in 1-shot learning, and enabling cross-task transfer from 0% to 31.2% without task-specific demonstrations. Our framework also demonstrates 2.4x faster convergence compared to existing online adaptation methods. Beyond LIBERO, Agentic-VLA retains its advantage on the dual-arm RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark, including under its randomized Hard setting. These results establish Agentic-VLA as a significant step toward truly adaptive VLA systems capable of continuous learning in deployment.

</details>


### [26] [Robots That Know What to Ask: Recovering Misaligned Rewards through Targeted Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22986)
*Helena Merker,Nick Walker,Andreea Bobu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种检测演示数据中特征未充分指定（underspecified features）的框架，通过分析特征在演示中的变异性来识别哪些特征未被充分示范，然后以自然语言向用户解释并请求针对性纠正演示，从而减少奖励函数学习中的歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从演示中学习奖励函数时，演示通常不完美：人类因认知负荷或物理难度可能弱化某些特征，或训练数据未能充分覆盖所有相关情境，导致重要特征未充分指定，学习到的奖励函数存在歧义，部署时产生行为偏差。

Method: 1）通过统计信号（特征在演示中的变异性）推断哪些特征被充分示范（低变异）或未充分指定（高变异）。2）机器人以自然语言解释不确定的特征，并向用户主动请求针对性的纠正演示。

Result: 在模拟桌面操作域和真实Franka机器人用户研究中，相比随机查询和被动数据收集，目标导向的、带解释的查询显著提升了奖励恢复效果，减少了不完美演示中持续存在的歧义。

Conclusion: 通过主动检测未充分指定的特征并请求针对性演示，可以有效解决奖励学习中因演示不完美导致的歧义问题，提升学习到的奖励函数与真实意图的对齐程度。

Abstract: Learning reward functions from demonstrations assumes that demonstrations provide adequate supervision over all features -- or task-relevant aspects of behavior. In practice, demonstrations are often imperfect: humans may under-emphasize certain features due to cognitive load or physical difficulty, or the training regime may fail to sufficiently cover all relevant situations. In either case, important features may be underspecified, leading to ambiguity in the learned reward function and misaligned behavior at deployment. We propose a framework that detects such underspecified features and actively solicits targeted corrective demonstrations. Our key insight is that demonstrations implicitly reveal which features are well specified: features that are consistently optimized show little variation across demonstrations, while features that are underspecified vary widely. We leverage this statistical signal to infer which features may have been insufficiently demonstrated. The robot then explains which features it is uncertain about in natural language and queries for demonstrations that explicitly address the identified gaps. We evaluate our approach in a simulated tabletop manipulation domain and in a user study with a real Franka robot. Targeted, explanation-guided queries significantly improve reward recovery compared to random querying and passive data collection, reducing ambiguity that would otherwise persist in learning from imperfect demonstrations.

</details>


### [27] [$π_0$-EqM: Equilibrium Matching for Closed-Loop Vision-Language-Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23128)
*Huanming Liu,Congsheng Xu,Jianmin Ji,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出π₀-EqM模型，用均衡匹配（EqM）解码器替换π₀中的流匹配专家，在300步预算下将RoboTwin平均成功率从40.4%提升至50.2%，LIBERO-10达到87.0%，并发现推理深度与任务成功率之间存在非单调的"平稳性-可执行性"差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型的流匹配动作解码器通常使用固定采样步数，限制了与状态相关的计算资源分配以及控制周期之间的时间复用能力，需要更灵活的解码策略。

Method: 将π₀中的流匹配专家替换为Equilibrium Matching (EqM)解码器，保持上游VLA堆栈不变，通过均衡匹配机制实现自适应推理深度。

Result: 在300步匹配预算下，π₀-EqM在19个任务上将RoboTwin平均成功率从40.4%提升至50.2%；在LIBERO基准上保持竞争力，LIBERO-10达到87.0%。通过两次阈值扫描发现残差与成功率之间存在任务依赖的非单调关系。

Conclusion: 迭代VLA控制中的推理深度应被视为策略设计的一部分；引入基于能量的VLA视角，为未来跨任务和跨本体的可组合动作生成研究提供新方向。

Abstract: Currently, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become the most adopted paradigm for robotic manipulation for its great potential for task generalization. While most generative flow-matching action decoders for VLA control are often deployed with fixed sampling horizons, limiting state-dependent compute and temporal reuse across control cycles. We present $π_0$-EqM, which replaces the flow-matching expert in $π_0$ with an Equilibrium Matching (EqM) decoder while leaving the upstream VLA stack unchanged. Under a matched 300-step budget, $π_0$-EqM improves RoboTwin average success from 40.4% to 50.2% across 19 tasks and remains competitive on LIBERO, with its clearest gain on LIBERO-10 (87.0%). Two threshold scans reveal a task-dependent non-monotonic relation between residual and success, which we term the stationarity--executability gap. The results suggest that inference depth in iterative VLA control is part of policy design and introduce an energy-based VLA perspective that may inform future work on composable action generation across tasks and embodiments.

</details>


### [28] [Semantic-Aware Guided Drone Exploration for Language-Conditioned 3D Indoor Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23160)
*Nitin Vegesna,Avideh Zakhor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAGE是一个面向未知3D室内环境的开放词汇探索系统，通过将CLIP语义信息集成到FALCON体积探索器中，在保持覆盖导向行为的同时实现语义重排序前沿选择，在仿真和真实环境中均比基线方法取得了更优的物体发现性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探索方法如FALCON在覆盖率方面表现良好，但缺乏语义感知能力，无法优先探索包含感兴趣物体的区域。如何在保持高效覆盖的同时引入语义先验知识来指导探索，是一个尚未解决的关键问题。

Method: SAGE在FALCON基础上集成了CLIP，包含四个关键组件：1）物体中心嵌入存储（object-centric embedding storage）；2）时间缓存（temporal cache），将近期观测投影到自由-未知边界；3）针对高相似度检测的物体前沿（object frontiers）；4）统一的语义-几何规划代价函数，通过约束语义重加权的权重来平衡语义引导和全覆盖。

Result: 在Matterport3D仿真中，SAGE在物体发现上优于FALCON和纯语义消融方法。与FTU相比，SAGE在9个共享地图-查询对上的探索速度快9.0~25.9倍，平均加速13.7倍，且体积吞吐量显著更高。在真实飞行实验中，FALCON探索更快、轨迹更短，但SAGE在物体发现上更优。

Conclusion: SAGE成功地将语义先验引入体积探索，在保持较好覆盖性能的同时显著提升了物体发现能力，验证了语义-几何联合规划的有效性。

Abstract: We present Semantic-Aware Guided Exploration, SAGE, a system for open-vocabulary exploration in unknown 3D indoor environments that preserves coverage-oriented behavior while allowing semantic cues to reprioritize frontier selection. Building on the FALCON volumetric explorer, SAGE integrates Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) via four key components: object-centric embedding storage, a temporal cache that projects recent observations onto the free-unknown boundary, object frontiers for high-similarity detections, and a unified semantic-geometric planning cost. This cost function bounds semantic reweighting influence, ensuring frontiers are prioritized without sacrificing total coverage. In Matterport3D-based simulations, SAGE outperforms FALCON and a semantic-only ablation in object discovery across map-query pairs. Compared to Finding Things in the Unknown (FTU), SAGE completes exploration 9.0 to 25.9 times faster across the nine shared map-query pairs, achieving a mean speedup of 13.7. Furthermore, SAGE achieves substantially higher volumetric throughput than FTU. Finally, we deploy SAGE in five real-world flights in two environments on a Modal AI Starling 2 quadrotor with onboard sensing and planning, and offboard CLIP inference. Comparing SAGE and FALCON, we find that while FALCON results in faster exploration and shorter mapping trajectories, SAGE outperforms FALCON in terms of object discovery.

</details>


### [29] [Autonomous Frontier-Based Exploration with VLM Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23165)
*Aarush Aitha,Avideh Zakhor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用视觉-语言模型(VLM)进行高层战略决策的自主机器人探索管线，通过多模态提示（地图+视觉图像）选择最优前沿，在仿真实验中覆盖率提升高达24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自主探索方法依赖简单的几何启发式规则选择前沿，缺乏上下文空间推理能力，难以应对未知和危险环境的复杂挑战。

Method: 构建两级管线：VLM在决策点接收当前地图和潜在路径（前沿）的视觉图像构成的多模态提示，执行高层战略决策选择最有前景的前沿；底层传统控制栈执行具体导航动作。

Result: 在六种室内环境仿真中，地图覆盖率相比现有方法提升高达24%。

Conclusion: 该管线轻量级、无需训练、可迁移至任何配备标准传感器和互联网连接的机器人，显著提升了自主探索性能。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic exploration of unknown and hazardous environments, a long-standing challenge, can be significantly improved by leveraging the advanced reasoning of Vision-Language Models (VLMs). We introduce a novel exploration pipeline where a VLM performs high-level strategic decision-making, guiding a conventional low-level robotics control stack. At decision points, the robot generates a multimodal prompt with its current map and visual imagery of potential paths, or frontiers. The VLM analyzes this prompt to select the most promising frontier, replacing simple geometric heuristics with contextual spatial reasoning. This approach, validated in simulation across six indoor environments, improves map coverage by up to 24\% over existing methods. Our pipeline is lightweight, training-free, and easily transferable to any robot with standard sensors and an internet connection.

</details>


### [30] [Signal Temporal Logic Motion Planning via Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23240)
*Yu Chen,Ancheng Hou,Mingyang Feng,Xiao Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将定时自动机与凸集图(GCS)相结合的连续时间运动规划框架，用于在信号时序逻辑(STL)规格下生成满足任务逻辑、时间要求及低层运动约束的光滑贝塞尔样条轨迹，并在低维基准、3D四旋翼、30自由度人形机器人和UR-3机械臂硬件实验中验证了其高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STL运动规划方法难以同时高效处理高维配置空间、连续时间平滑性要求和复杂逻辑时序约束的组合问题。

Method: 首先将STL规格转换为定时自动机，再与配置空间的凸分解耦合构建联合转移系统；然后将STL运动规划问题转化为凸集图上的最短路径问题，求解得到光滑贝塞尔样条轨迹。

Result: 在低维基准、3D四旋翼、30自由度人形机器人和UR-3机械臂硬件实验中，该方法能够高效求解复杂STL运动规划问题并生成光滑可执行轨迹。

Conclusion: 所提框架是完备的，且当定时自动机和凸分解固定后，凸松弛的复杂度随配置空间维度和贝塞尔阶数呈多项式增长，具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: This paper investigates continuous-time motion planning under Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications. The goal is to generate smooth robot trajectories that satisfy high-level logical and timing requirements while respecting low-level motion constraints. To this end, we propose an efficient framework that combines timed-automata reasoning with graphs of convex sets (GCS). An STL specification is first represented by a timed automaton, which is then coupled with a convex decomposition of the configuration space to form a joint transition system encoding both task progress and region occupancy. Based on this joint transition system, the STL motion-planning problem is reformulated as a shortest-path problem over a GCS, whose solution induces a smooth Bézier-spline trajectory satisfying the STL specification, smoothness requirements, and velocity bounds. We establish the soundness of the proposed formulation and analyze its computational complexity, showing that, once the timed automaton and convex decomposition are fixed, the convex relaxation scales polynomially with the configuration-space dimension and the Bézier degree. We further develop a compact timed-automaton construction for an expressive STL fragment using dedicated templates and Boolean composition. Numerical experiments on low-dimensional benchmarks, a $3$-D quadrotor, a $30$-DoF humanoid, and a hardware experiment on a UR-3 robot arm demonstrate that the proposed method efficiently solves complex STL motion-planning problems and produces smooth executable trajectories.

</details>


### [31] [Turning Adaptation into Assets: Cross-Domain Bridging for Online Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23257)
*Zixuan Hu,Xuantuo Huang,Yancheng Li,Yichun Hu,Shengyong Xu,Ling-Yu Duan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 IDEA 框架，通过 Fisher 加权软提示优化和动态资产库构建跨域桥梁，解决 VLN 中测试时适应的灾难性遗忘和负迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLN 的测试时适应方法将在线适应视为短暂的、孤立的更新，导致灾难性遗忘和负迁移，无法应对非平稳环境变化。

Method: 1) 通过 Fisher 加权方案优化软提示捕获可迁移知识；2) 将优化后的提示与域坐标结合形成动态资产库；3) 利用资产库将目标域投影到历史知识凸包上构建跨域桥梁；4) 资产库与桥梁形成互补循环。

Result: 在 REVERIE、R2R 和 R2R-CE 基准上一致优于现有方法，实现无需训练的资产共享适应。

Conclusion: IDEA 通过将适应转化为资产的积累与组合，有效解决了灾难性遗忘和负迁移，为 VLN 在非平稳环境下的部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Navigating under non-stationary environment shifts poses a critical challenge for a Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) agent deployed in the wild. Yet, existing Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) methods for VLN largely treat online adaptation as transient, isolated updates, leading to catastrophic forgetting and negative transfer. To overcome these issues, we propose Inter-Domain BridgE with Historical Assets (IDEA), a novel TTA framework that transforms adaptation into the accumulation and composition of assets. Specifically, IDEA introduces soft prompts optimized via a Fisher-guided weighting scheme to capture the transferable knowledge. These optimized prompts are then augmented with domain coordinates to form a dynamic asset library. Leveraging this library, IDEA constructs a cross-domain bridge by projecting the target domain onto the convex hull of historical knowledge. These designs form a complementary loop: the evolving library underpins bridge construction, while the bridge provides superior initialization to accelerate asset optimization. Extensive experiments across REVERIE, R2R, and R2R-CE benchmarks demonstrate the consistent superiority of IDEA over existing methods, showcasing its ability to enable training-free adaptation via asset sharing.

</details>


### [32] [6G Communication Networks Enabling Embodied Agents: Architecture and Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23263)
*Lipeng Dai,Luping Xiang,Kun Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了面向具身智能体的6G通信系统，提出了包含人类意图感知层、O-RAN传输层、智能中介层和具身层的分层通信架构，并通过端到端原型验证了毫秒级延迟与稳定闭环控制的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体对通信的延迟、可靠性、异构性要求远高于纯软件智能体，而现有研究对如何利用6G能力支持具身智能体通信缺乏系统性探讨。

Method: 首先辨析具身与非具身智能体的概念差异，分析具身智能体与6G的共生关系；然后提出四层分层通信架构（人类意图感知层、O-RAN传输层、智能中介层、具身层）；最后基于5G O-RAN测试床、触觉设备、机械臂等搭建端到端原型进行实验验证。

Result: 原型实验实现了毫秒级延迟和稳定的闭环控制，验证了所提架构的可行性与实用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层架构为未来6G-具身智能体通信研究和工业部署提供了有效参考。

Abstract: Embodied agents, which couple intelligent decision-making with physical actuation in the real world, impose far more stringent and heterogeneous communication requirements than purely software-based agents. While 6G promises sub-millisecond latency, ultra-high reliability, native intelligence, and integrated sensing, systematic studies on how to exploit these capabilities for embodied agent communication remain limited. This article investigates 6G-enabled communication systems for embodied agents from both conceptual and engineering perspectives. First, we review the concept, embodiment value of embodied agents, and clarify their distinctions from disembodied agents. Then, we analyse the symbiotic relationship between embodied agents and 6G networks. We highlight how key 6G enablers can support the stringent requirements of human-robot interaction. Furthermore, we demonstrate the proactive role of embodied agents in bolstering communication networks through coverage extension, environmental sensing, and physical world understanding. Building on these insights, we propose a hierarchical communication architecture for human-robot remote interaction, comprising a human-intent perception layer, an open radio access network (O-RAN)-based transport layer, an intelligent intermediary layer, and an embodiment layer. To validate its feasibility, we implement an end-to-end prototype that integrates a haptic device, an industrial robotic arm, an intermediary platform, and a 5G O-RAN testbed. Experimental results demonstrate millisecond-level latency and stable closed-loop operation, confirming the practicality of the proposed architecture and providing a reference for future 6G-embodied agent research and industrial deployments.

</details>


### [33] [Sparse Compositional Flow Matching by geometric assembly from motion primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23341)
*Yan Tang,Yuanbo Tang,Tingyu Cao,Shaolun Huang,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于流程匹配的组合式轨迹生成框架，通过运动基元字典学习和结构稀疏流程匹配，直接在物理轨迹空间进行组合生成，在Open X-Embodiment和3DMoTraj数据集上达到SOTA，FDE/ADE比率从1.8降至1.07，ADE和FDE分别提升19.2%和21.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型将具身轨迹视为稠密的整体信号逐点生成，拟合复杂高维后验的同时忽略了数据的潜在组合结构，导致样本效率低下。作者认为组合式潜在结构是自然选择：具身任务共享可复用的运动基元，且组合单元与子任务边界自然对齐。

Method: 设计两个耦合模块：(1) 运动基元字典学习——为每个原子配备可学习长度掩码和二进制起始指示器，使原子本身即基元，可逐字复用；(2) 结构稀疏流程匹配与几何约束——通过时长感知分词和可微几何损失生成二进制放置矩阵，确保相邻基元连接处的空间连续性和时间连续性。

Result: 在Open X-Embodiment和3DMoTraj数据集上达到SOTA，将FDE/ADE比率从1.8降至1.07，ADE提升19.2%，FDE提升21.0%。

Conclusion: 直接在物理轨迹空间进行组合式生成优于潜在空间组合方式，通过显式建模运动基元的组合结构可显著提升轨迹预测的精度和样本效率。

Abstract: Embodied trajectories, such as the executable motion sequences of robotic manipulators, underwater vehicles, and mobile robots, are a fundamental output of embodied AI. Modern generative models often treat them as a dense, monolithic signal generated point by point, fitting an intricate high-dimensional posterior while leaving the data's latent structure unmodeled, the same sample inefficiency long identified by the structured generative model literature. We argue that a compositional latent structure is a natural choice: many embodied tasks share recurring motion fragments that can be made explicit as a finite repertoire of reusable motion primitives, and compositional units naturally align with subtask boundaries to support task decomposition. Existing compositional generators, however, compose in a latent space and rely on post-hoc decoding to relate sampled units to actual trajectory segments. We instead compose directly in the physical trajectory space through a flow-matching framework with two coupled designs. Motion-Primitive Dictionary Learning equips each atom with a learnable length mask and binary starting indicators so the atom itself is the primitive, reused verbatim wherever it is placed. Structural Sparse Flow Matching with Geometric Constraints then generates a binary placement matrix using duration-aware tokenization and a differentiable geometric loss that enforces spatial continuity and temporal contiguity where adjacent primitives meet. On Open X-Embodiment and 3DMoTraj, the framework attains state-of-the-art accuracy and reduces the FDE/ADE ratio from 1.8 to 1.07, improving ADE by 19.2% and FDE by 21.0% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [34] [Multi-Floor Exploration for Ground Robots via an Incremental Reachable Graph and Structural Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23350)
*Zhiwen Zhu,Jiaqi Chen,Xiangyi Huang,Meiqi Hu,Boyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于增量可达图的多楼层自主探索框架，通过稀疏图表示重叠可通行表面，结合假设图初始化和分层规划器，实现了高效的多楼层探索与建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统2D和2.5D地图无法表示楼梯、坡道等多层重叠可通行表面，导致地面机器人在多楼层建筑中自主探索困难。

Method: 构建增量可达图（稀疏图）表示可通行支撑面；通过试探性图元素维护潜在连接；投影已探索楼层的任务区域先验初始化目标楼层的假设图；分层规划器联合推理确认和假设结构进行全局引导。

Result: 仿真中相比基线方法提高了探索效率和建图完整性；实物实验验证了实际可行性和实时性能。

Conclusion: 提出的多楼层探索框架有效解决了重叠可通行表面的表示问题，具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration of multi-floor buildings remains challenging for ground robots because conventional 2D and 2.5D maps cannot represent overlapping traversable surfaces such as stairs, ramps, and multiple reachable elevations. This letter presents a multi-floor exploration framework based on an incremental reachable graph. Built as a sparse graph over reachable support surfaces, the graph preserves potentially valid connectivity through tentative graph elements under sparse observations and enables stable, physically reachable frontier detection. To guide exploration beyond the currently mapped floor, we project task-zone priors from an explored floor to initialize a hypothetical graph on the target floor and reconcile it incrementally with incoming observations. A hierarchical planner then jointly reasons over confirmed and hypothetical structures for global guidance. In simulation, the proposed method demonstrates improved exploration efficiency and mapping completeness compared to evaluated baselines. Furthermore, onboard real-world experiments validate its practical feasibility and real-time performance.

</details>


### [35] [Droneulator: A Portable UAV Simulator for Agricultural Workflows with RotorPy and Godot 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23386)
*Jacob Swindell,Michael Lowen,Marija Popovic,Riccardo Polvara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Droneulator是一个结合RotorPy多旋翼动力学和Godot 4渲染的便携式无人机模拟器架构，通过Zenoh-based ROS~2兼容管道发布同步视觉和状态流，支持PX4控制及WebSocket命令，可在同一基础设施上实现面向农业无人机的图像采集、局部规划和强化学习实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业无人机研究需要集成真实3D场景、高保真车辆动力学和机器人中间件的模拟器，同时需在异构开发机器上易于部署，现有方案难以兼顾这些需求。

Method: 将RotorPy多旋翼动力学引擎与Godot 4渲染和传感器生成相结合；通过Zenoh协议构建ROS~2兼容的同步视觉与状态流管道；同时暴露PX4控制接口和轻量级WebSocket命令路径。

Result: 在树尺度图像采集（COLMAP 3D重建）、冠层障碍物局部规划（EGO-Planner）和闭环强化学习（Gymnasium环境）三个农业无人机工作流中验证：低延迟感知、不同采集密度下的重建数据收集、无碰撞局部规划、基于深度感知的障碍物避让策略训练均达到稳定性能。

Conclusion: Droneulator展示了在同一可部署架构中支持农业无人机检测、规划和学习任务的潜力。

Abstract: Agricultural UAV research requires simulators that integrate realistic 3D scenes, high-fidelity vehicle dynamics, and robotics middleware, while remaining practical to deploy across heterogeneous development machines. We present Droneulator, a portable UAV simulator architecture that combines RotorPy for multirotor dynamics with Godot 4 for rendering and sensor generation. Droneulator exposes both PX4-based control and a lightweight WebSocket command path, and publishes synchronised visual and state streams through a Zenoh-based ROS~2-compatible pipeline. This integration enables a single stack to support inspection-oriented data capture, ROS~2/PX4 local planning, and reinforcement learning experiments without modifying the simulator infrastructure. We present quantified validation of the current system across three agricultural UAV workflows: tree-scale image collection for 3D reconstruction with COLMAP, local planning around canopy obstacles using EGO-Planner, and closed-loop reinforcement learning through a custom Gymnasium environment. In the reported setup, the results show that the simulator can sustain low-latency sensing, support reconstruction-oriented data collection under varying capture density, execute collision-free local planning around canopy obstacles, and support stable depth-sensing-based policy training for obstacle-aware navigation. Together, these results show the potential of Droneulator for agricultural UAV inspection, planning, and learning within one deployable stack.

</details>


### [36] [Vision-Based Agile Landing on Turbulent Waters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23717)
*Dimosthenis Angelis,Leonard Bauersfeld,Davide Scaramuzza,Evangelos Boukas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的无人机自主着陆方法，无需显式平台状态信息，使用视觉特征和机体状态预测控制指令，在模拟和真实实验中均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上无人机着陆面临飞行器与平台耦合运动的挑战，现有方法依赖显式平台状态信息，在恶劣海况下可靠性不足。

Method: 采用强化学习策略，输入为多旋翼机体状态测量值和从着陆表面提取的局部视觉特征（关键点和描述子），输出姿态和推力指令，由底层控制器跟踪。策略在仿真中用合成关键点和随机归一化描述子训练，实现零样本迁移。

Result: 在仿真中优于MPC基线方法，尤其在"Very Rough"海况下；真实实验中成功使用两种不同的局部特征提取器实现自主机载着陆。

Conclusion: 这是首个无需显式平台状态表示的敏捷多旋翼无人机海上着陆方法，证明了基于视觉特征和强化学习的方案在恶劣海况下的可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous landing of Unmanned Aerial Vehicles on maritime vessels is challenging due to the coupled motion of the vehicle and landing platform in open-sea conditions. This paper presents a reinforcement-learning-based approach for autonomous multirotor landing on moving maritime platforms without requiring explicit platform-state information. The proposed method uses multirotor state measurements together with local visual features, consisting of keypoints and associated descriptors extracted from the landing surface, to predict attitude and thrust commands. These commands are tracked by a conventional low-level controller. The policy is trained in simulation using synthetic keypoints with randomly generated normalized descriptors, enabling zero-shot deployment with different local feature extractors onboard the UAV. We evaluate the method in a realistic simulator and show that it outperforms a state-of-the-art Model Predictive Control baseline under platform motions corresponding to ``Very Rough'' sea conditions. Finally, we perform extensive real-world experiments, demonstrating autonomous onboard landing using two different local feature extractors. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first approach for agile multirotor landing on maritime platforms in turbulent waters that does not rely on an explicit platform-state representation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法依赖几何重定向或间接动态重定向，这些中间运动学投影引入了几何偏差，限制了搜索空间并产生了次优的动态行为，需要一种能直接从专家视频生成动态可行轨迹的方法。

Method: 提出直接动态重定向（DDR）单阶段框架，在任务空间中利用物理仿真器内的基于采样的模型预测控制（MPC）求解器，直接优化复杂接触序列并减轻输入漂移，绕过中间几何投影的偏差。

Result: DDR在示踪追踪精度上优于当前最先进的基线方法，并且为强化学习智能体提供的物理可行参考能加速训练收敛，提升敏捷和平衡动作的最终执行效果。

Conclusion: 绕过几何偏差的直接动态重定向方法能生成更高质量的轨迹，并有效促进下游强化学习训练，是实现人形机器人从人类视频中高效学习复杂技能的有效途径。

Abstract: Imitation Learning from monocular video demonstrations provides a scalable approach for teaching complex skills to humanoid robots. However, translating human motion to humanoids requires overcoming significant morphological mismatches. Standard approaches rely on Geometric Retargeting or Indirect Dynamic Retargeting pipelines. We identify that these intermediate kinematic projections introduce a geometric bias, restricting the search space and yielding suboptimal dynamic behaviors. In this paper, we propose Direct Dynamic Retargeting (DDR), a novel single-stage framework that generates high-fidelity, dynamically feasible trajectories directly from expert videos. By formulating the problem in the task space and leveraging a sampling-based Model Predictive Control solver within a physics simulator, DDR natively optimizes over complex contact sequences while mitigating input drift. Our experiments demonstrate that bypassing the geometric bias allows DDR to outperform state-of-the-art baselines in demonstration tracking accuracy. Furthermore, we establish that providing such physically viable references to RL agents accelerates training convergence and enhances the final execution of agile and balancing behaviors. Source code will be made publicly available.

</details>
